Update
Yageo Corp.  | Asia Pacific
TDK-Taiyo Yuden MOU: high-
end wall rises; cycle thesis
intact
The MOU validates AI-driven high-cap MLCC tightness, which
supports pricing and our MLCC up-cycle view.
TDK and Taiyo Yuden (both covered by Shoji Sato) signed an MOU to explore an
alliance in advanced MLCCs/inductors –  including manufacturing/procurement
cooperation, potential METI subsidies under Japan's Economic Security Act, and a
possible future capital tie-up.

Our take: We estimate MLCC demand next year at more than 40bn units of 47uF+,
which crowds out  midrange/low-end MLCC capacity.
1. We believe  de-specing to ≤22uF MLCCs is not a viable workaround.
Substituting high-cap MLCCs with larger counts of lower-capacitance parts
requires broad real estate that simply does not exist on today's densely
packed GPU/accelerator boards.
2. Switching to tantalum-polymer capacitors merely trades one shortage for
another, given equally tight tantalum supply.

3. So the only realistic industry response, in our view, is reallocating midrange/
low-end MLCC capacity toward high-cap production. That would structurally
tightens midrange/low-end supply, underpinning broad-based pricing power.
Things to watch:
• Whether a capital alliance materializes,
• METI subsidy size (this determines how distorted the high-end cost curve
gets),
• Counter-move by Murata (also covered by Shoji Sato) counter-move, and
• Any sign the alliance extends into mid-cap products.

See our latest report on MLCC here: Global Technology: MLCC: Continued Demand
Growth for High-Value-added Products for AI & Data Centers (15 Sep 2026)

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Yageo Corp. (2327.TW)
Base case, multistage residual income valuation model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 7.8%
(risk-free rate 1%, equity risk premium 6.8% and beta of 1.1), medium-term growth rate 12%,
terminal growth rate 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected revenue/profit recognition from stronger AI server/notebook
demand
n Further price hikes led by stronger pricing power arising from better demand and/or

tighter supply
Risks to Downside
n A sudden bursting of demand for AI
n Inventory risk
n Consumer electronics demand risk

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
In this report, we focus on final JMTBA orders, which we believe
could be among the catalysts for Airtac's and Hiwin's share
prices.
Per the Japan Machine Tool Builders' Association (JMTBA), headline orders grew
2% MoM and 65% YoY in August 2026 (vs. -5% MoM/+50% YoY in July 2026), to
¥198bn.
• Domestic orders  -3% MoM/+62% YoY (vs.-8% MoM/+50% YoY     in July), to
¥52bn
• Overseas orders  +5% MoM/+66% YoY (vs. -4% MoM/+51% YoY   in July), to

¥146bn
• By region:
° China: -2% MoM/+62% YoY, to ¥47bn
° Europe: -12% MoM/+44% YoY, to ¥20bn
° North America: +24% MoM/+72% YoY, to ¥50bn
Our view:
• We think the solid YoY growth for overseas orders continues to point to an
upcycle scenario, despite some seasonal volatility  with MoM growth in some
regions,
• On top of overall segment recovery within automation:
° Airtac (1590.TW, OW) should benefit from its own share gains and
incremental contributions from linear motion components. Valuation

appears undemanding at 17x 2027e P/E (vs. 25x average since 2020).
° Hiwin (2049.TW, OW) should show margin expansion in the coming
quarters, supported by higher utilization and price hikes. We see
potential share price upside as the stock now trades at 29x 2027e P/E
(vs. peak cycle valuation of 35-40x).
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
September 30, 2026 05:11 PM GMT

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple

of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.
n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Telecom & Networking Equipment  | North America
Potential FCC Rules on Optical
Transceivers More Likely to
Come in at 3.2T
Recent DC meetings highlight 3.2T as the most likely generation
to see restrictions on China-based optical transceivers. This
echoes conversations with LITE during the Q noting that any
restrictions would need to be phased in. See potential rules
keeping laser market tight, a pos for LITE/COHR.
Morgan Stanley & Co. LLC

Morgan Stanley MUFG Securities Co., Ltd.+
Yu Shirakawa
Equity Analyst
Yu.Shirakawa@morganstanleymufg.com                                                +81 3 6836-5432
Telecom & Networking Equipment
North America
Industry View In-Line
What Happened: Morgan Stanley strategists recently  met with a senior Washington
telecom, FCC and export-control legal team with direct engagement across the
White House and FCC. Discussion focused on the Administration’s efforts to reduce

reliance on Chinese technology, with the FCC Covered List emerging as the more
likely near-term mechanism for optical transceivers (a note we made in our AI Safety
DC trip wrap-up last week). Policy discussions suggest 3.2T transceivers would likely
be where restrictions would be phased in, potentially as soon as October, with
industry recently asked to demonstrate credible U.S. or friend-shoring plans before
3.2T becomes broadly deployed. Our conversations noted that discussions are also

indicating Chinese-built transceivers could still be importable, even at 3.2T, as long
as 65% of the BOM comes from US-based companies (DSP / laser would get you
pretty close). Initial 3.2T volumes are not expected to ramp in volume until 2029,
limiting the near-term revenue impact of a restriction even if action comes sooner.
We would see these rules as having the potential to avoid creating a constraint
on hyperscalers' ability to build today, but keeping the  laser market tighter given

laser would largely need to be from a US-based vendor to meet a 65% value
threshold, a positive for LITE/COHR.
How this echoes conversation with LITE earlier in the quarter: We hosted LITE at
Nasdaq Asia in late August, where we asked the company about potential FCC rules.
At the time they noted that the FCC had been in contact with them about potential
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Recent DC trip by our strategist   team gave a greater idea of what potential FCC
rules on optical transceivers could look like.
Potential restrictions, likely to come in at 3.2T, limit Chinese-based transceivers,
with a potential workaround if US-based content above 65% of the BOM.
Solution seemingly a win-win for LITE/COHR, with laser market likely to stay

tight, but not having to take on lower module assembly business.
Also, does not create bottlenecks for hyperscalers.
Assuring InP substrate delivery will be key to look at going forward.
October 1, 2026 04:09 AM GMT

### Page 2

M
Update
2
rules, with the company noting their feedback was that the rules would need to be
phased in over time to prevent material supply/demand dislocation in the market
with 60-65% of transceivers coming from China-based transceiver vendors. They
also noted that pure transceiver volumes alone, at current pricing structures, would
not be the most attractive business for them to bring on. These rules, which are
both phased in and don't limit Chinese assemblers, as long as enough content is

US-based, would seemingly address both of these concerns.
Our Thoughts:
1. 3.2T looks like the likely policy pressure point, but the near-term impact
should be limited - Administration efforts appear focused on reshaping the
supply chain before 3.2T reaches volume rather than disrupting the existing
800G and 1.6T base. With the 3.2T ramp not expected to begin until 2028
and broader adoption closer to 2029, an October Covered List action would
provide roughly two years before meaningful volumes enter the market.

Hyperscalers are already working on plans intended to demonstrate greater
U.S. or friend-shored manufacturing, suggesting the policy objective is
increasingly about determining where the next generation is built rather
than immediately displacing current Chinese supply.
2. Laser pricing looks like the stronger read-through versus transceiver
share - A Covered List framework does not necessarily require Chinese
module vendors to lose significant share if they can increase the U.S.

content of the underlying BOM. Combining a U.S. DSP from Marvell with
laser content from LITE/COHR could potentially move a Chinese-built
transceiver to the 65% U.S.-value threshold discussed in our strategists
team policy meeting. Such an outcome would leave Innolight and Eoptolink
(both covered by Andy Meng) with less flexibility to substitute toward
China-based lasers while allowing them to remain relevant at the module
level, reducing the risk from China-based laser makers. We therefore see a

stronger read-through for high-speed laser pricing and LITE/COHR
component share than for a wholesale redistribution of transceiver share.
3. Replacement capacity not ready; nor is it the most attractive opportunity
- As noted above, LITE noted that assembly of transceivers (mid-30's to low-
40s gross margin product), is not the most attractive opportunity for US-
based optical companies (vs. EMLs and lasers well north of 50% steady
state and approaching 75% today). While 3.2T is not yet in production, we

use AAOI's current 800G and 1.6T capacity ramp as an illustration of the
scale of US supply available to absorb a potential future demand shift. AAOI
(not covered), the only material US-based production of transceivers, is
currently ramping capacity. AAOI exited Q2 with manufacturing capacity
approaching 200,000 800G and 1.6T units per month and expects to reach
~650,000 units per month by year-end 2026, while management continues
to expect demand to outpace production through mid-2027. AAOI’s YE26

capacity annualizes to ~12M 800G/1.6T units, versus ~101M units
combined for Innolight and Eoptolink based on annualized 1H capacity
(44.3M and 56.7M, respectively), or >8x AAOI. Based on AAOI’s year-end
capacity plans and our Internet team’s hyperscaler capex work, we estimate
AAOI could support less than 4 GW of hyperscaler capacity even before

### Page 3

M
Update
Morgan Stanley Research 3
factoring in manufacturing utilization. Replacing a meaningful portion of
Innolight and Eoptolink’s supply would therefore require a substantial multi-
year capacity build, with limited ability for existing U.S. suppliers to absorb
those volumes in the near term.
4. InP supply and sourcing outside of China will still be areas to watch -
Potential FCC rules could create a win-win for LITE/COHR by reducing the
risk of China-based laser makers flooding the market, without forcing them

to build significant transceiver module capacity to absorb lower-margin
assembly business. Were China to view the rules as too one-sided, it could
respond with tighter restrictions on InP substrate exports, a key bottleneck
to laser capacity ramping. At the same time, sourcing outside of China could
broaden the beneficiary set beyond U.S. suppliers, supporting InP substrate
demand at JX Advanced Metals (5016 JP), while providing a tailwind for
Sumitomo Electric’s (5802 JP) CWLD products and Furukawa Electric’s

(5801 JP) DFB lasers.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over
the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.

Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.

年台灣營收占比為 77％、香港為 13%，美國佔 10%。品牌營收別八方雲集佔
74.7%，梁社漢佔 24.8%，芳珍佔 0.1%，丹堤 0.4%。1H26 台灣獲利貢獻 107%，
美國為虧損，競爭同業為王品、瓦城、美食、揚秦。

高肉品存貨，全球採購以舒緩豬價上漲的壓力。雖然今年以來包材、油品、牛肉等
產品價格上漲，但 1H26 豬肉價格下滑，支撐台灣 OP 從 1H25 的 14.7%提升到
1H26 的 16.85%。 
美國德州中央工廠和首家門市營運：美國 2025 年有 13 家據點，德州達拉斯延遲
至 2026/9 開幕(原為 2026/4)， 以快餐方式出餐的店型 ，透過提前預制縮短出餐時
間增加來客數，提高點餐的便利性吸引非亞洲族群消費，年底前德州將再展 2 家
門市。目前除了加州有中央工廠，德州中央工廠已於4 月完工啟用，可供應30-50
家門市，長期將取代加州中央工廠的功能，以降低加州中央工廠的費用分攤，短期
之內仍將呈現虧損。 
財務預估： 2026 年預估台灣營收 73.3 億元 (+7%YoY)，美國營收 9.6 億元
(+46%YoY)，預估 2026 年營收 95.2 億元(+8%YoY)，稅後獲利預估 9.7 億元
(+13%YoY)，今年配股 0.5 元，以除權後股本7 億元計算，稅後EPS 預估為 13.82
元。2027 年預估台灣營收 76.1 億元(+4%YoY)，美國營收 12.5 億元(+30%YoY)，
預估 2027 年營收 100.8 億元(+6%YoY)，稅後獲利預估 10.3 億元(+6%YoY)，稅
後 EPS 預估為 14.7 元。

## Document 093: 3234光環 第一金

Source PDF: all report pdf/3234光環 第一金.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-10-05
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

周建霖 
(886-2)2563-1666 
mark.chou@firstsec.com.tw

光環(3234) 
買進, TP 240 (潛在報酬 32.23%)  
買進 
Price(10/02):181.5 
Target Price：240

在完成客戶 5,000 小時可靠度測試認證後，光環代工業務自 26H2
起重新貢獻營運， 隨稼動率提升及產品組合優化， 本業獲利已顯著
改善並順利轉虧為盈 。 為配合客戶後續產能規劃， 公司亦將斥資約
11 億元進行產線擴充，預期至2027 年底產能規模將達 2026 年底
的 3 倍。此外，針對具 Scale-up 應用潛力的 VCSEL 技術，光環
亦規劃推出 1060nm VCSEL 方案供客戶驗證，有望切入新一代
AI 光互連市場，進一步強化公司中長期競爭優勢。綜合以上，研
究部預估光環 2026 年 EPS 0.57 元，2027 年 EPS 6.77 元，目前
PE 為 26.5x，參考光通上游同業 PE 評價為 20-60x，考量光環本
業獲利將開始由虧轉盈且顯著成長，且也開始佈局 Scale-up 雷射
方案，因此給予 35xPER，目標價 240 元，建議買進。 
⚫ 公司基本資料 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
光環(3234)成立於 1997 年，主要產品為光通訊主動元件，包含雷
射晶粒切割、次模組封裝，2025 年晶粒及零組件佔 63%、光電轉
換件 28%、其他 9%。應用領域則為頻寬網路 10%、4G/5G 基地
台互聯 31%、雲端數據中心 22%、感測應用 26%、其他 11%，銷
售地區為台灣 60%、 中國8%、 歐洲及美洲17%、 亞洲及其他15%。 
圖一：產品組合

殖利率(%) - - ⚫ 營運概況 
取得客戶測試認證，代工業務重回貢獻 
光環 26Q2 營收 2.0 億元，Q+7.5%，Y+28.1%，毛利率 19.82%，
稅後淨利-1744 萬元，Q 持續虧損，Y 持續虧損，EPS -0.16 元。
在重新取得客戶 5000 小時的測試認證下，代工收入逐步重回貢獻
且產品組合優化下，使整體毛利率回升至雙位數以上，並在費用控
管得宜讓本業獲利虧損顯著收斂。

代工事業加速成長，本業由虧轉盈 
光環 8 月營收 1.1 億元，M+13.1%，Y+110.1%，7 月營收 9604
萬元，M+25.2%，Y+96.9%，7-8 月合計 2.0 億元，Y+103.7%。
代工事業逐月增加片數 ， 研究部認為在稼動率回升及產品組合優化
下，光環獲利將持續獲得顯著改善，其中7 月自結稅後淨利約 900
萬，EPS 0.08 元，反映出本業隨營收的放大而已轉虧為盈。綜合
以 上 ， 研 究 部 預 估 光 環26Q3 營收 3.2 億元， Q+63.9% ，
Y+113.2%，毛利率 33.77%，稅後淨利 4462 萬元，Q 虧轉盈，Y
虧轉盈，EPS 0.35 元。

產能積極布局，同時開發 1060nm VCSEL 自有產品 
光環今年以來已完成 16.5 億元現金增資(稀釋 EPS 約 11.8%)，並
公告將斥資約 11 億元進行產能擴充及設備擴置。研究部推估，此
次擴產主要係配合客戶 2027～2028 年產能規劃 ， 因而提前建置相
對應代工設備，預期至 2027 年底整體產能規模有望達 2026 年底
的 3 倍，且公司亦已著手規劃 2028 年後續擴產計畫。綜上所述，
預期光環代工業務將自 26H2 起加速成長，隨產品組合改善，本業
可望轉為穩定獲利，加以產能利用率提升帶動營運槓桿逐步顯現，
預期整體獲利增幅將優於營收成長。此外，光環深耕VCSEL 市場
多 年 ， 而 今 年 以 來 多 家 國 際 光 通 訊 及 雷 射 大 廠 亦 陸 續 提 出 以

3 
VCSEL 作為 Scale-up 光互連光源的方案 。 相較SiPh 方案 ，VCSEL
具備高頻寬密度、低功耗、低延遲潛力及高可靠度等優勢，過往傳
輸 距 離 較 短 的 限 制 ， 在 以 機 櫃 內 及 機 櫃 間 短 距 離 互 連 為 主 的
Scale-up 應用中影響亦相對有限。研究部認為，若未來 Scale-up
架構朝 Wide-and-Slow 方向發展，VCSEL 將成為極具競爭力的技
術選項。光環憑藉既有技術與量產經驗，目前也開始佈局1060nm
的 VCSEL 方案，預計於年底推出樣品給客戶測試，未來有望成為
強化公司長期競爭優勢的自有產品。綜合以上，研究部預估光環
2026 年營收 11.0 億元 ，Y+60.7%， 毛利率28.29%， 稅後淨利7233
萬元 ，Y 虧轉盈 ，EPS 0.57 元；2027 年營收 28.8 億元 ，Y+162.0%，
毛利率 40.89%，稅後淨利 8.6 億元，Y+1083.9%，EPS 6.77 元。

圖二：Scale-up 互連技術比較

資料來源：Lumentum、第一金投顧彙整

結論 
在完成客戶 5,000 小時可靠度測試認證後，光環代工業務自 26H2
起重新貢獻營運，隨稼動率提升及產品組合優化，本業獲利已顯著
改善並順利轉虧為盈。為配合客戶後續產能規劃，公司亦將斥資約
11 億元進行產線擴充， 預期至2027 年底產能規模將達 2026 年底
的 3 倍。此外，針對具 Scale-up 應用潛力的 VCSEL 技術，光環
亦規劃推出 1060nm VCSEL 方案供客戶驗證 ， 有望切入新一代AI
光互連市場，進一步強化公司中長期競爭優勢。綜合以上，研究部

事欣科(4916 TT)  
台灣股市︱電子下游 
投 資 速 報 
強力買進-首次 目標價 NT$155 (潛在報酬+51.2%) 2026-09-29

LEO＋航太 /國防雙引擎   
◼ 結 論 與 建 議 
展望 2H26~2027 年， 2Q26 本 業 表 現 強 勁 ， 上 半 年 EPS 創 歷史 新高 。 8 月
營收 持 續 成 長，成 長 動 能 以 低 軌 衛 星 及 航 太 國防 為主 ，公司 已 通過 ITA R（國
際 武 器 貿 易 條 例 ）與 AS 9100 航 太 品 質 認 證 。受惠 在 手 訂 單 逾 1.35 億 美 元，
其中 航 太 國 防 ＋ 低 軌 衛 星 合 計 佔 過 半，且 持 續 向 高 毛 利 應 用 集 中。 預估 2027
年 稅 後 EPS 6.17 元， PE 約 16.6 X， 考 量 反 無 人 機 系 統 急 單 持 續 ， 客 戶 要 求
產 能 加 倍 ， LEO 隨 客 戶 新 發 射 計 畫 推 進 ， 建 議 強 力 買 進 ， 目 標價為 155 元
(25 * 2027 年 EPS) 。

◼ 重點摘要  
公 司 簡 介  
公司是 台 灣 上 市 的 電 子 製 造 服 務 （ E MS） 與 原 始 設 備 製 造 （ OE M） 公 司 ，
專 注 於 高 混 、 中 低 量 產 的 嵌 入 式 系 統 與 精 密 組 裝 。 主要業務 包括 a. 航太／
國 防 ／ 低 軌 道 衛 星 （ 目 前 營 收 主 力，約 佔 較 高 比 重 ） ：線 束 （ca ble h arn ess ）、
機 箱 佈 線 組 件、鈑 金、電 纜 訂 製、 UL 安 全 帶、精 密 組 裝 等，具 備 A S9100D 、
ITA R 等 認 證 ， 能 在 美 國 加 州 與 內 華 達 州 生 產 敏 感 軍 事 組 件 。 b. 博 弈 ／ 資 訊
娛 樂 ： 博 弈 板 卡 、 準 系 統 、 玩 家 追 蹤 系 統 、 機 台 框 體 等 。 外 銷 佔 比 極 高（ 約
99% 以 上 ， 美 洲 為 主，約 98% ） ， 內 銷 極 低 。 近 年 航 太 ／ 國 防 ／ 低 軌 衛 星
比 重 提 升 ， 博 弈 與 工 業 電 腦 仍 佔 重 要 地 位 。 ITA R 認 證 與 美 國 在 地 生 產 形 成
軍 工 航 太 護 城 河 ； 受 惠 供 應 鏈 去 中 化 與 「 美 國 製 造 」 趨 勢 ； 導 入 AI 自動化
提 升 效 率 ， 毛 利 率 目 標 中 長 期 維 持 20% 以上。

低 軌 衛 星 為 主 要 成 長 動 能  
1H26 營收 YOY+100% ， 產 品 為 衛 星 本 體 上 的 ca ble h a r n es s（ 面 板 電 源 、
推 進 器、 鏡 頭 等 ） ， 輕 量 化、 抗 輻 射， 客 戶 AS TS 今 年 已 發 射 約 12 – 13 顆，
目標 2026 年 發 射 45 – 60 顆 衛 星 ； 並 切 入 衛 星 關 鍵 電 子 通 訊 模 組 試 產 訂
單 ， 預 計 4Q26 正 式 出 貨 ， 毛 利 率 > 平 均 ， 27F 營收 YOY+50% 。  
a. 核 心 產 品 ： 衛 星 本 體 上 的 高 可 靠 度 cable harn ess （ 面 板 電 源 、 推 進 器 、
鏡 頭 、 手 臂 等 ） ， 非 地 面 接 收 器 。 需 符 合 航 太 等 級 規 格 ， 是 衛 星 關 鍵 部
件。  
b. 客戶 ： 包括 供應 (S pa ceX Star link 相 關 ） 衛 星 本 體 線 束 與 地 面 接 收 站 IPC
相 關 產 品 ；新 客 戶 AST S pa ceMobile （ A STS ）2026 年 6 月隨 BlueBird
衛 星 成 功 發 射， 公 司 已 取 得 下 一 世 代 低 軌 衛 星 關 鍵 電 子 通 訊 模 組 試 產 訂
單 ， 預 計 4Q26 正 式 出 貨 。 若 驗 證 順 利 ， 後 續 量 產 訂 單 可 望 大 幅 爆 發 。  
圖 二 、 未 來 衛 星 發 射 數 量 預 估

資 料 來 源 ： 萊 德 光 電 -KY 、 康 和 投 顧 整 理 ， 2026/9

航 太 ／ 國 防 產 品 穩 定 貢 獻  
1H26 營收 YOY+60% ， 包 括 單 兵 穿 戴 裝 置 、 反 無 人 機 飛 彈 系 統 ， 無 人 機 攝
像 系 統 等；航 太 大 廠（ 含 波 音 ）無 人 機 訂 單 持 續 追 加， 3Q26 起 已 實 際 拉 貨 ，
需 求 已 排 至 2027 年， 27F 營收 YOY+30% 。  
主 要 產 品 包 括 ： a. 軍 用 穿 戴 裝 置 : 用 在 作 戰 人 員 胸 前 ／ 手 腕 式 裝 置 （ 亦 可 延
伸 至 消 防 、 警 察 、 救 援 ）； b. 反 無 人 機 系 統 ： 因 美 伊 相 關 衝 突 導 致 客 戶 庫 存
大 量 消 耗 ， 急 單 湧 現 ， 要 求 加 大 產 能 、 最快速度出貨 ； c.  無 人 機 控 制 相 關
電 腦 零 件 ： 2025 年 切 入 波 音 無 人 機 供 應 鏈 ， 2026 年 開 始 正 式 放 量 ； 目 前
小 量 後 階 段 ， 實 際 大 量 拉 貨 自 Q3 起 ， 需 求 已 排 至 2027 年 ； d. 海 事 監 控 無
人 機 與 建 控 系 統 、 攝 像 系 統（ 標 準 偵 測 距 離 20 公 里 、 長 程 30 公 里 以 上 ） 。 
核心 優 勢 ： 與 美 國 國 防 大 客 戶 合 作 超 過 16 年 ， 近 期 獲 頒 優 良 供 應 商 獎 。 公
司 可 提 供 從 PCBA 到 完 整 機 電 整 合 、 cable harn ess 的 整 套 解 決 方 案 ， 整 合
多 供 應 商 能 力 ， 協 助 客 戶 降 本 並 保 留 毛 利 。

受 惠 低 軌 衛 星 及 航 太 / 國防 帶 動 ， 預 估 2027 年 營 收 持 續 成 長  
2Q26 營收 13.77 億 元 ， 季 增 約 30.2% 、 年 增 約 76.3 %。 主 因 國 防 軍 工 與 低

軌 衛 星 產 品 出 貨 強 勁 。 毛 利 率 20.7 % ， 較 前 一 季 提 升 約 0.5 個 百 分 點 、 年
增約 0.5 個 百 分 點 ， 表 現 穩定 ， 稅 後 淨 利 1.19 億元 ， YOY+2542.1% ，稅
後 EPS 0.96 元。 累 計 營 收 年 增 約 48.6% ，歸 屬 母 公 司 淨 利 年 增 逾 9 倍，E PS 
5.4 4 元 超 越 歷 年 全 年 度 水 準 ， 刷 新 上 市 以 來 歷 史 新 高 。  
訂 單 能 見 度 ： 截 至 2026 年 8 月底 預估 約 1.35 億 美 元 ， 其中 航 太 ／ 國 防 佔
約 40% 、 通 訊 （ 含 低 軌 衛 星 ） 約 18% 、 博 弈 約 17% 。 低 軌 衛 星 為 重 要 成 長
引 擎 （ 主 要 客 戶 已 發 射 衛 星 ， 預 計 年 底 擴 增 ， 新 一 代 衛 星 體 積 放 大 帶 動 線
束 需 求 ） 。公 司 已 斬 獲AS T S pa ce  Mobile 關 鍵 零 組 件 試 產 訂 單，預 計 4Q26
出 貨 。  
產 能 規 劃 ： 墨西哥 廠 製 造 產 品 回 美 國 享 零 關 稅 （ USMCA 認 證 ）， 有 效 規 避
關 稅 風 險 。 另 外 ，美 墨 雙 據 點 可 彈 性 配 置 ， 高 自 動 化 ／ 高 機 密 產 品 留 美 國 ，
勞 動 密 集（ 如 線 束 ）移 墨 西 哥 ， 優 化 成 本 與 交 期 。 目 前 利 用 率 約 7 成 左 右 ，
在 新 訂 單 帶 動 下 ， 預估 後續 營收 有 機 會 倍增 。  
整 體 來 看 ， 2026 年 1~8 月 營 收 35.08 億 元 ， YOY+ 61.8 % ， 8 月 營 收 創 新
高 ， 第 三 、 四 季 為 傳 統 專 案 交 付 旺 季 ， 國 防 、 航 太 、 低 軌 衛 星 動 能 延 續 。
公 司 管 理 層 看 好 下 半 年 營 運 優 於 上 半 年 ， 全 年 本 業 強 勁 成 長 ， 毛 利 率 可 望
維持 20 ~21 % 左右，獲利顯著優於過往年度。另積極布局無人載具等新領
域。 長 線 預 期 衛 星 成 長 趨 勢 明 確 ， 2027>2026>2025 年 ， 相 關 訂 單 動 能 持
續 。 預 估 2027 年營收為 78.11 億 元 ， YoY+ 30% ， 在 自 動 化 導 入 與 產 品 組
合 優 化 後 ， 預估 維 持 在 20% 上 下 浮 動 2 個 百 分 點 ， 稅 後 淨 利 為 7 . 6 6億元，
YOY -23.8 % ， 稅 後 E PS 為 6.17 元。

評等說明  
1)  強力 買進 / Str on g Bu y ： 我 們 對 該 評 價 個 股 未 來 展 望 正 向 ， 並 預 估 於 未 來 12 個 月 投 資 期 間 ， 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 大 於 50 % 
2)  買進 / Bu y： 我 們 對 該 評 價 個 股 未 來 展 望 正 向 ， 並 預 估 於 未 來 12 個 月 投 資 期 間 ， 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 大 於 25%  
3)  逢低買進 /Tr a din g Bu y ： 我 們 對 該 評 價 個 股 未 來 展 望 正向 ， 並 預 估 於 未 來 12 個 月 投 資 期 間 ， 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 小 於 25%  
4)  未評等 / N ot Ra te d ： 至 目 前 我 們 沒 有 足 夠 基 本 資 料 判 斷 該 公 司 評 等 ； 或 此 期 間 康 和 與 該 公 司 有 特 定 交 易 ， 基 於 規 範 或 康
和 政 策 ， 限 制 予 該 公 司 評 等

免責宣言 :  
本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 康 和 投 資 顧 問 股 份 有 限 公 司 版 權 所 有 ， 禁 止 任 何 形 式 之 抄 襲 、 引 用 或 轉 載 。 本 研 究 報 告 所 載 內 容 ， 純 屬
研 究 性 質 ， 所 載 述 意 見 可 隨 時 予 以 更 改 或 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 ， 僅 提 供 康 和 集 團 內 部 同 仁 及 客 戶 參 考 ， 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何
意 見 ， 不 構 成 ( 且 不 應 解 釋 為 ) 在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 劵 或 其 他 投 資 標 的 之 要 約 、 招 攬 、 邀 請 、 宣 傳 、 誘 使 ，
或 任 何 不 論 種 類 或 形 式 之 表 示 、 建 議 或 推 薦 買 賣 本 研 究 報 告 所 述 的 任 何 證 劵 。 報 告 內 容 所 提 及 之 各 項 業 務 、 財 務 等 相 關 資
訊 、 意 見 及 預 估 ， 係 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資 料 來 源 ， 既 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 做 出 任
何 陳 述 或 保 證 ， 且 報 告 係 基 於 特 定 日 期 所 做 之 判 斷 ， 有 其 時 效 性 限 制 ， 如 有 變 更 ， 本 公 司 將 不 做 預 告 或 更 新 。 對 於 本 投 資
報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 劵 、 投 資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 於 決 策 時 應 審 慎 衡 量 本 身 風 險 ， 並 就
投 資 結 果 自 行 負 責 。

## Document 108: 7856漢測_20261008_UBS

Source PDF: all report pdf/7856漢測_20261008_UBS.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-10-08
Pages: 27
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 8 October 2026
Global Research
Initiation of Coverage
Hermes Testing Solutions
Shifting-left, scaling-up: dual catalysts in chip
probing and CPO
Jack-of-all-trades for bespoke testing solutions in the semiconductor industry
We initiate coverage of Hermes Testing (HTSI) with a Buy rating and NT$8,880 PT. HTSI is
uniquely positioned to benefit from two key semiconductor testing trends: shift-left
testing and the emerging CPO testing market. Leveraging its long-standing relationship

with TSMC, HTSI has established capabilities across electrical test engineering,
customised test equipment, thermal control modules, probe cards, and CPO testing
solutions. We forecast revenue growth of 127%/152%/78% in 2026E/27E/28E, driven
by increasing adoption of thermal control modules for wafer-level test and initial
deployment of CPO insertion 2 and insertion 3 test equipment through ficonTEC.
Additional 2028E growth drivers include reflow tools, laser clean solutions, and MEMS

probe cards. The stock trades at 27/15x 2027/28E PE, versus ~35x NTM PE for handler-
equipment peers, offering re-rating potential as sales scale, new products are qualified,
and its CPO presence expands. See our companion testing deep dive sector note.
Shift-left beneficiary with exceptional thermal control modules
Thermal control modules should be HTSI's largest growth driver over the next several
years. We expect increasing chip power consumption, rising package complexity, and

tighter probe pitches to drive adoption of advanced thermal control solutions used
alongside leading-edge probers, in additional to more wafer level insertions and longer
test time. We estimate HTSI will ship approximately 3,000 thermal-control modules in
2027E, up from around 1,200 units in 2026E, sales contribution rise from ~30% in
Q226 to >50% of sales by 2027E. The launch of its second-generation platform in
2027E, which carries 25-50% higher ASPs, should further improve profitability. We

forecast GM expansion from 55.5% in 2026E to 58.3%/60.0% in 2027E/28E.
Unique position to capture CPO upside
Through its partnership with German optical-automation leader ficonTEC, HTSI is
positioned to participate in the initial ramp of CPO testing equipment. HTSI supports
local manufacturing, installation, automation and after-sales service, making it a key
enabler of volume production. We estimate HTSI captures approximately 30% of
equipment value across the 40 insertion 2 and 70 insertion 3 systems we forecast for

delivery in 2027E, with further potential upside in 2028E as adoption expands.
Valuation: initiate coverage with a Buy rating and price target NT$8,880
We forecast EPS of NT$29.1 in 2026E (including a one-off employee share purchase
programme impact), rising to NT$153.3/286.5 in 2027E/28E. Our NT$8,880 price target
is based on 31x average 2028E PE, versus handler-equipment peers forward PE at
roughly 35x, reflecting HTSI's unique exposure to both shift-left testing, CPO, and our

estimate of 32% LT earnings CAGR. We initiate with a Buy rating.

### Page 2

Hermes Testing Solutions UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Will HTSI be able to keep its moat within chip probing thermal control modules?
Yes. We expect HTSI's competitive position to strengthen as chip power consumption, package
complexity, and probe density continue to increase. The company is developing next-generation
thermal control solutions, including liquid cooling technologies, while also expanding into specialized

chuck solutions designed to address wafer warpage. In addition, HTSI's close collaboration with
TSMC, TEL, Advantest, and other OSATs should reinforce its position within advanced wafer-level
testing.
Q: Will insertion 2 of CPO testing process be skipped?
No. We believe concerns surrounding insertion 2 adoption are overdone. While some industry
participants favor bypassing insertion 2 due to throughput and ROI considerations, doing so increases
the risk of defective optical engines being identified only after the components are delivered to

OSATs, creating yield accountability challenges and higher packaging losses. Based on our industry
studies, we expect insertion 2 testing to remain an important part of the CPO workflow over at least
the next two years as the industry focuses on improving PIC yields and overall testing efficiency.
We initiate coverage of Hermes Testing (HTSI) with a Buy rating and NT$8,880 price target. HTSI
offers unique exposure to two of the semiconductor testing industry's most attractive growth drivers:

1) shift-left testing and 2) CPO testing. The industry's shift-left testing strategy will have more test
insertions move to the wafer level before packaging, in addition to longer CP time. Leveraging its
longstanding relationship with TSMC, HTSI has established capabilities across thermal control
modules, electrical-test engineering services, customized equipment, and emerging CPO testing
solutions. We believe there could be re-rating potential as sales scale, new products are qualified, and

its CPO presence expands.
TSMC and OSATs have been expanding their CoWoS capacities aggressively, from 40kwpm in 2024
to around 90 kwpm in 2025, and is tracking to expand CoWoS capacity toward 160kwpm in 2026E.
TSMC also ramped up expanding N3/N2 capacity, with multiple product launches from customers'
pipelines—Nvidia's Rubin, AMD's MI400 series/Venice CPU and other cloud ASICs—translating into
foreseeable, strong testing demand.
Despite a 264% YTD share-price increase, HTSI currently trades at just 15x of our 2028E EPS versus

handler-equipment peers forward PE at roughly 35x. We believe market expectations for 2027 CPO
testing equipment shipments remain conservative, particularly for insertion 2 equipment. Based on
our industry analysis, we estimate HTSI will deliver 40 insertion 2 and 70 insertion 3 systems in 2027E,
this should be higher than the market's expectation of no insertion 2 and limited insertion 3 shipment
due to engineering bottleneck. We also see further upside in 2028E as CPO adoption expands. Our

reverse DCF analysis indicates that the current market price implies only a 17% long-term sales CAGR
vs our estimate of 27% and long-term GM level of 33% vs our estimate of 61%. We see scope for
re-rating.

Hermes Testing Solutions UBS Research
Testing TAM expanding with IC complexity: leaders likely to
outperform industry growth
Our central view is that testing content per AI accelerator is growing faster than
semiconductor unit volumes, creating a multi-year expansion in testing TAM. We believe
semiconductor testing is entering a period of structural growth as AI-driven chip
complexity increases testing intensity across wafer, package and system levels. The

industry's shift-left strategy, which moves more testing upstream in the semiconductor
manufacturing process, is resulting in longer and more frequent test insertions. As cloud
AI GPUs and ASICs become increasingly complex, additional testing is required to ensure
functionality, performance and yield, while reducing defective parts per million (DPPM).
More robust testing should improve deployment efficiency and lower the cost of
operating AI infrastructure.

Semiconductor testing generally comprises four stages: wafer/chip probing (CP), final
test (FT), system-level test (SLT), and burn-in. CP is conducted at the wafer-level before
packaging, while FT, SLT, and burn-in occur after packaging. The below Figure 3S e m i c o n d u c t o r  t e s t i n g  p r o c e s s :  f r o m  w a f e r  l e v e l  t o  p a c k a g e  l e v e l
illustrates the semiconductor manufacturing flow.
Figure 1: Semiconductor testing process: from wafer level to package level

Source: UBS
We estimate semiconductor testing historically accounts for ~1% of total
semiconductor revenue. Within testing, consumables such as probe cards, sockets and
interface boards represent ~10-15% of industry revenue. According to Yole, the probe
card, socket and interface board markets should grow from US$3.8bn, US$2.3bn and
US$1.7bn in 2026 to US$5.5bn, US$3.1bn and US$2.0bn by 2031, respectively.

Nonetheless, leaders within the testing sector will standout among competition, with
greater sales CAGR comparing to the industry.
We see HTSI's sales for the next few years to mainly be driven by 1) thermal control
module shipment and 2) CPO equipment shipment. In the past, thermal control

modules weren't necessary for chip probing, due to less TDP for the chips. However, as
new iterations of AI accelerators now got substantial power draw, this unlocks a brand
new TAM for HTSI to enter with their thermal control modules to pair with probers, in
order to prevent probe card damages.
 Robust thermal control module shipments.  We estimate HTSI to ship
3,000/4,100 thermal control modules in 2027E/28E, with continuous demand in
future years, bringing their 2026-31E semiconductor equipment sales CAGR to

48%.

### Page 4

 Thermal control module ASP expansion.  Gen 2 thermal control modules will
start to ship in 2027E which will drive tool ASP up around 25-50%.
 Demand supported by Advantest expansions. We believe the 3,000 thermal
control modules shipment forecast for 2027E is not far-fetched, as we read-across
from Advantest's announced capacity expansion plan (testers will be paired with
probers with a 1:1 ratio) with increasing capacity up to 5,000 testers within

FY2027/3 and they also announced a 10,000-unit target within CY2028.
Although the testers capacity expansion plan includes testers for final test, we do
see a good portion to be allocated to chip probing given shift-left testing trend
increasing both testing insertions and testing time before chip packaging.
 CPO is another brand new TAM for semiconductor testing.  As insertion 2
and 3 testing equipment demand starts to rise in 2027 with scale-out CPO

adoption, we see HTSI to ship 40/70 insertion 2 and 3 equipment in 2027E, with
this number growing in 2028E to 80/160 and higher in future years as CPO
proliferates, and driving 52% 2026-31E test equipment & engineering services
sales CAGR.
 CPO to see content increase. Gen 2 insertion 3 equipment may start to ship in
2027E, with higher throughput and tool ASP .
 Insertion 2 might scale down, but not happening anytime soon. Insertion 2
equipment shipment might come down in 2029E and beyond, as foundries scale-

up yield for photonic IC (PIC) in order to lessen the time consuming insertion 2
testing burden.

Robust growth drivers across semiconductor testing
We believe HTSI is set to benefit from several semiconductor testing growth drivers and
capture the AI induced, expanding testing TAM. We see HTSI is uniquely positioned as
the main beneficiary of two of the strongest growth drivers within semiconductor
testing: testing shift-left and CPO.
Shift-left igniting secular growth for chip probing

### Page 5

The industry's adoption of shift-left testing should drive a multi-year structural growth
opportunity for chip probing (CP) and its supply chain. As advanced packaging costs rise
and AI accelerators become increasingly complex, semiconductor manufacturers are
placing greater emphasis on identifying known-good dies (KGD) before packaging.
Figure 4: CP is set to grow substantially with shift-left, each packaging step set to be MORE costly than the prior step

RDL / RWAT testing Wafer-level CP (WLCP)
CoWChiplet (if use SoIC) CoW
CP (Die A)
CP (Die B)
Assembly Test (AST)
oS
Source: UBS
The economics of advanced packaging reinforce this trend. Heterogeneous integration,
chiplet architectures and 2.5D/3D packaging significantly increase package value, while
defects identified late in the manufacturing process can lead to substantial scrap costs.
To mitigate this risk, foundries and OSATs such as TSMC and ASE employ distributed

testing flows incorporating KGD, known-good-stack (KGS) and assembly test screening,
to catch defects before the components enter a multi-week packaging cycle (>2 weeks
for CoW and 2 weeks for oS). The importance of these tests rises as AI clusters become
increasingly dependent on component reliability and low defective-parts-per-million
(DPPM) rates. Given the multiplicative yield degradation, a 300bps yield decline at just
one manufacturing stage (e.g. dropping from 99.5% to 96.5%) reduces overall

integrated four-stage yield from 98.0% to 95.0%; on a 500,000 unit ramp at US
$10,000 per unit, this 3% scrap delta translates into around US$150m in unrecoverable
losses given >6-month manufacturing lead times.
At the same time, rising chip power consumption is increasing the technical difficulty of
wafer-level testing. As chip thermal design power scales beyond 1,000W and power
density is expected by the industry to surge another 8x by 2030, traditional wafer-level

chip probe (WLCP) encounters severe physical bottlenecks. Finer probe pitches and
higher power densities increase the risk of overheating, probe needle burnout and yield
degradation, creating demand for more advanced probing and thermal-control
solutions.

Hermes Testing (HTSI) is a key beneficiary of this trend. Leveraging its long-standing
relationship with TSMC and expertise in semiconductor test solutions, HTSI supplies

advanced thermal control modules used alongside leading-edge probers (made by
Tokyo Electron), particularly for AI applications. These thermal control modules are
required for high TDP semiconductor wafer probe in order to maintain the temperature

### Page 6

of the chuck to avoid needle burns or probe card damages. We estimate HTSI will ship
approximately 3,000 thermal-management modules in 2027E, up from 1,200 in 2026E,
supporting sales growth of 127%/152% in 2026E/27E. HTSI is also the partner for
prober installation, prober maintenance and services. In addition, HTSI provides high-
end chuck processing services, an area that should benefit from increasing package sizes
and higher wafer warpage associated with advanced packaging.

Beyond thermal control modules, HTSI also supplies consumables and customised
solutions for wafer-level testing, including probe card cleaning sheets and laser-based
probe head cleaning systems. As probing intensity increases under the shift-left testing
model, demand for cleaning and maintenance solutions should rise alongside probe
card utilisation. Currently, cleaning sheets is around 5% of sales, but we believe it could
gain traction in 2027/28E and beyond. We see additional upside from future modular

laser cleaning systems that could be integrated directly with probers, further expanding
HTSI's content opportunity within advanced wafer-level testing.
CPO testing fuelling future growth for semiconductor testing
As interconnect bottlenecks increasingly shift from the memory wall to the copper wall,
we believe co-packaged optics (CPO) adoption is inevitable over the longer term. Testing
remains one of the key bottlenecks to CPO deployment, as optical validation differs

fundamentally from the electrical testing processes that have dominated semiconductor
manufacturing for decades. As a result, CPO is creating a new testing opportunity across
the semiconductor equipment and consumables ecosystem. We have written
extensively on CPO and opportunities that it brings to the semiconductor supply chain,
see our CPO deep dive , our thoughts on CPO timing , 2026 SiPhIA conference
takeaways, and SEMICON 2026 takeaways.
Various test equipment suppliers are scrambling to come up with ideal testing

equipment across the four insertions of CPO testing, with solutions to ensure both
optical and electrical signal integrity of the CPO solutions. The four insertions are:
 Insertion 1: Electronic IC (EIC)/Photonic IC (PIC) test.  Single-side wafer test
that has dedicated probe cards to test the fabricated EIC/PIC wafers.
 Insertion 2: Optical/Electrical test. Double sided wafer test, testing the hybrid
bonded (e.g. via TSMC's COUPE) EIC and PIC wafers by an opto-electrical test that

verifies the electrical signals on the top and optical signal on the bottom of the
hybrid bonded wafers.
 Insertion 3: Optical engine (OE) test.  The OE tests are done to ensure the
quality of the OEs and with FAU (Fiber Array Unit) attached, to ensure the signal
integrity of the OEs. The testing equipment needs to be able to handle 224G per
channel data rates, requiring high unit per hour (UPH) throughput, meticulous dirt
mitigation, and maintaining a near 100% contact rating on high-speed sockets.

 Insertion 4: ASIC final test.  This final test step is established post the OEs are
packaged together with the ASIC. The complete ASIC package (now with CPO) is
now tested for opto-electrical functionalities.
As it stands, insertion 2 and insertion 3 are the two areas where there are the most
debates in the industry. Insertion 3 suppliers such as Chroma has noted the trend of
“shift-to-middle” paradigm centered on insertion 3. In high-density scale-out switches

deploying up to 32 optical engines (e.g. Nvidia’s Spectrum-X architectures), any OE
defect results in package scrap at the CoWoS level, making 100% known good die
(KGD) validation at the singulated OE stage an absolute commercial requirement.

With its 25 years of precision test equipment expertise, ficonTEC is an early mover for
CPO test equipment and has key know-how for optical alignment, which is
quintessential for optical test. HTSI, through its partnership with ficonTEC, is set to be a
major beneficiary once CPO proliferates in 2027E-28E. As the partner of ficonTEC,
Hermes Testing is the key enabler that can help ficonTEC achieve HVM for its CPO
testing equipment. As of now, our view is that both insertion 2 and insertion 3 should be

required within the next two years, despite the difficulties surrounding double-sided
wafer probing (insertion 2), as the yield for OEs are still not high enough to avoid
defective dies. Finding out known-good OEs will be crucial before the FAU coupling
process during the latter stages of CPO packaging, to prevent impeding the final
package with multiple OEs attached to the switch ASIC (e.g. 32 OEs on Nvidia's
Spectrum-X). We estimate Hermes Testing will ship 40/70 units of insertion 2/3 testing

equipment in 2027E, with potential upside to the numbers as we view Hermes Testing
will likely assist with the ramp up for Gen 2 insertion 3 equipment in 2027E. Gen 2
insertion equipment will come with better throughput and ASP .

through customised prober solutions for photonic integrated circuit (PIC) testing. We
believe these solutions could eventually be packaged as licensable intellectual property,

creating an additional long-term growth opportunity as CPO adoption broadens. While
near-term testing growth is driven by AI accelerators, CPO represents the next major
expansion cycle for semiconductor testing. We believe HTSI is uniquely positioned to
participate across multiple test insertions, providing exposure to a potentially significant
source of incremental revenue beginning in 2027E.

HTSI's unique position for new TAM of thermal control
modules and CPO test equipment
Hermes Testing Solutions (HTSI) was founded in 2004 in Hsinchu, Taiwan, as a spin-off
from Hermes-Epitek. The company provides semiconductor testing and inspection

solutions, engineering services, customized equipment, and test consumables. HTSI
maintains a global service footprint across Taiwan (Hsinchu, Tainan), Mainland China
(Suzhou), Japan (Tokyo, Kumamoto), Singapore, Malaysia, and the United States
(Arizona), enabling close support for leading semiconductor customers.
Its main business segments are structured around three pillars:
 Semiconductor equipment and customized products : HTSI utilises a dual-

track strategy of independent R&D and also equipment agency sales. They offers

### Page 9

highly customized solutions designed to address specific client pain points and
bottlenecks. Key products include: thermal control modules that pair with a 1:1
ratio with wafer-level test probers, electron beam inspection equipment, white
light interference optical inspection systems, laser probe cleaning machines, and
the SYMPHONY fluxless reflow system.
 Testing equipment engineering services:  The company operates the largest
engineering service team in Taiwan, consisting of over 340 technical professionals.

The engineering service team provides extensive equipment life-cycle support,
including installation, relocations, modification, testing, routine maintenance, and
repair services. CPO related equipment sales/services will be recognized within this
segment, as CPO are not direct HTSI products, but rather sales recognition via its
partnership with ficonTEC.
 Probe card design, manufacturing, and cleaning materials:  HTSI delivers a
comprehensive product portfolio that supports the entire probe card life-cycle. For

probe cards, they supply membrane, cantilever, and vertical probe cards. As they
are small in scale, HTSI targets niche probe card applications such as RF, 5G/6G,
millimeter wave, LCD driver, low end logics, TIA (Transimpedance Amplifier), etc. In
addition to the three existing probe technologies, HTSI also collaborates with MJC
in Japan for MEMS probe development, targeting sales in 2028E for high-end, AI/
HPC related applications. Cleaning materials is another important business within

this segment, as HTSI source and process sand paper or other materials to offer
cleaning sheets to clean the probe cards, removing oxidations, unwanted particles
and substances that accumulated on the probe cards during the testing process.
The sales resiliency in the coming years mainly stems from its robust relationship with key
industry players, especially as an engineering and service provider with TSMC for testing
solutions. This sets up a solid foundation for its customized equipment business, as they

know the specific testing bottleneck to tackle. For instance, as Hermes-Epitek
manufacturers the probers for Tokyo Electron (TEL) and HTSI helps with installation,
services, and maintenance of the probers, they understand the thermal bottlenecks that
the wafer-level test industry has been facing and can effectively come up with module
solutions to solve this.
Margins set to expand with value-added products and services
The company has demonstrated strong execution in advanced wafer-level testing, with

H126 revenue of NT$2.2bn already exceeding 90% of FY25 revenue (NT$2.4bn).
Profitability has also improved significantly, with GM increasing from 42.1% in 2025 to
54.9% in H126.
With share of value-added products and services set to rise in 2027E in sales mix, HTSI is
set to see GM and operating leverage expansion. As a niche engineering service provider
that has robust ties to leading foundries and OSATs, we believe HTSI has substantial
pricing power and estimate HTSI' GM for thermal control modules to be around 65%,

with GM for CPO equipment of around 60% as HTSI provides an engineering-service-
like business for it. We believe these factors will help the company to reach a 60% long-
term GM range.
OPM is also set to expand with customized equipment sales rise in total sales mix. If we
exclude the one-time expense to be recognized during Q326 for employee stock
benefits (we estimate around NT$1bn), YoY opex growth will be around 99% for
2027E, with downside risk if the company conducts tight cost control measures. We

forecast the company's OPM will expand from 19.5% in 2026E, to 44.3%/47.0% in
2027/28E.

Source: LSEG, UBS
Despite a 264% YTD share-price increase, HTSI still trades at just 15x 2028E P/E. We
believe market expectations for 2027E CPO testing equipment shipments remain
conservative, particularly for insertion 2 equipment. Based on our industry analysis, we
estimate HTSI will deliver 40 insertion 2 and 70 insertion 3 systems in 2027E, this should
be higher than the market's expectation of no insertion 2 and limited insertion 3
shipment due to engineering bottleneck. We also see further upside in 2028E as CPO

adoption expands. Our reverse DCF analysis indicates that the current market price
implies only a 17% long-term sales CAGR vs our estimate of 27% and long-term GM
level of 33% vs our estimate of 61%. We see scope for re-rating.
We believe the market is not fully reflecting three key growth drivers:
1) Shift-left testing is still underappreciated.
As advanced packaging costs rise and AI accelerators become increasingly complex,
more thorough testing is done at the wafer-level. This should drive sustained demand

for HTSI's thermal control modules, probe card consumables, and other wafer-level
testing solutions.
2) CPO revenue potential remains underestimated.
Investor expectations for 2027 CPO equipment shipments, particularly insertion 2
testing systems, remain conservative. Based on our industry studies, we forecast HTSI
will deliver 40 insertion 2 and 70 insertion 3 systems in 2027E, with further potential
upside in 2028E as CPO adoption broadens.
3) New products provide additional optionality.

Beyond its core businesses, HTSI is expanding into higher-value solutions including mass-
reflow tools, MEMS probe cards and laser cleaning systems, creating incremental
growth opportunities that we believe are largely absent from current expectations.
We value HTSI using 31x average 2028E PE, underpinned by a 32% 2027-31E earnings
CAGR. We use 2028E PE as our view is that 2028 is the year for thermal control modules
and CPO adoption grow from the initial ramping up stage to mass adoption stage. We

believe using handlers as a peer group is justifiable for a unique company like HTSI, with
thermal control modules for chip probing sharing similar characteristics as active thermal
control used for final test, in addition to Hon Precision and Chroma having exposure to
CPO test equipment. For equipment vendors, the peer group's average NTM PE post
2022 is around 35x, and the group is currently trading at around 41x NTM PE (see
What's Priced In chart).

HTSI is trading at NT$4,190 as of 6
Oct 2026

Source: UBS estimates
Risk to the current share price is heavily skewed (2.2:1) to the upside.
UPSIDE (NT$10,000): Our upside scenario reflects stronger than expected thermal
module and CPO equipment shipment in 2027-28E, with additional upside from better
traction of probe cards and mass reflow tools, forming 157%/91% YoY sales growth in
2027/28E and even higher operating leverage with OPM of 46.3%/50.0% in 2027/28E.

This scenario implies a 34x 2028E PE, a upside driving by more equipment demand
stemming for robust shift-left test induced equipment bottleneck, alongside CPO
proliferation with scale-up CPO equipment pull-in in 2028E.
BASE (NT$8,880): Our price target of NT$8,880 is based on 31x 2028E PE. We expect an
earnings CAGR of 32% in 2027-31E. With shift-left trend for more thermal module
requirement during testing, alongside 40/70 CPO insertion 2/3 shipment. We see these

growth drivers driving YoY sales growth from a high base of 127% in 2026E to
152%/78% YoY in 2027/2028E, with OPM of 44.3%/47.0% in 2027/28E.
DOWNSIDE (NT$1,500): Our downside scenario reflects lower shipment of thermal
control modules due to less advanced prober demand with less insertion 2 and 3
equipment shipment due to bottlenecks with CPO production, alongside limited
additional growth drivers to be picked up in 2028 such as MEMS probe card and mass

reflow tools. This leads to weaker YoY sales growth of 67%/50% in 2027/28E and
operating margin of 29.3%/32.0% in 2027/28E as equipment shipment fails to scale.
This scenario implies a 14x 2028E PE, as valuation de-rate with dimmed outlook without
CPO and limited upside from thermal control module shipments.

Company Description
Company Description
Hermes Testing Solutions (HTSI) was founded in 2004 in Hsinchu,
Taiwan. It focuses on providing semiconductor testing and
inspecting solutions, equipment installation, technical support,
and maintenance services. Its main business segments are
customized semiconductor equipment, engineering services, and
probe cards and other materials.
Industry Outlook
New iterations of semiconductors, especially for AI / HPC

applications, are set to increase in both front-end wafer
fabrication advancements and back-end packaging complexities
with each iterations. With the industry now moves to utilize
"shift left", chip probing and its relevant supply chain will be key
beneficiaries of this trend which we believe will unlock multi-
year, secular growth trends.

Company Description
Hermes Testing Solutions (HTSI) was founded in 2004 in Hsinchu, Taiwan. It focuses on
providing semiconductor testing and inspecting solutions, equipment installation, technical
support, and maintenance services. Its main business segments are customized
semiconductor equipment, engineering services, and probe cards and other materials.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Hermes Testing based on PE methodology.
Risks for the semis industry:
- Geopolitical uncertainties: Geopolitical uncertainties including tariffs and more export
control could negatively impact semis cycle, and a downturn would lead to slower demand in
the overall market and moderating capacity expansion.
- Meaningful deceleration of new technology development: Slower demand and less
investment are expected If there is meaningful deceleration of new technology development

such as Cloud AI, autonomous driving, etc.
- Rising utility cost: Larger-than-expected utility cost increases would further pressure
profitability. Rising utility costs in Taiwan have negatively impacted margins for Taiwan tech
companies.
Company risks:
- Faster- or slower-than-expected front-end/CoWoS capacity expansion: This could result in
Hermes Testing gaining/losing equipment orders from major foundries and OSATs.
- Competitors achieving technological advancement: Potential risk of competition (from

either local or foreign companies) achieving technological advancements could result in
Hermes Testing losing market share from major foundries and OSATs.
- Better- or worse-than-expected AI growth: Better- or worse-than-expected AI growth could
lead to a ramp-up or a slowdown of equipment orders from major foundries and OSATs to
Hermes Testing, affecting long-term revenue growth potential.
- Slowdown or delay of new technological advancement: A potential slowdown or delay of

the rollout for technological advancements for technologies such as SoIC, CoPoS, and CPO,
would limit future equipment orders.
- Increase of raw material costs: Rising raw material costs may directly impact Hermes
Testing’s margins for equipment shipments.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

宏捷科 (8086 TT)  Trading Buy

2H26 展望保守 ， 但1H27 有機會轉佳 ， 評等為Trading Buy 。 
投資建議 ：由於磊晶片、黃金等原材料成本偏高，宏捷科 2H26
獲利表現不易較 2Q26 明顯改善，不過，待新機種相關產品陸續
上市後，預估 1H27 毛利率有機會回升，獲利展望亦得以轉佳，
故群益對宏捷科的投資建議為 Trading Buy 。  
2Q26 稅後淨利 2.78 億元， QoQ-6.23% ：受惠於手機相關產品
出貨持續暢旺， 2Q26 宏捷科營收 12.98 億元， QoQ+6.23% ，不
過 ， 受到磊晶片 、 黃金等原材料漲價影響 ， 毛利率降低至29.98% ，
營業利益 QoQ-13.40% ，稅後淨利 2.78 億元， QoQ-6.23% 。  
2H26 獲利展望仍保守，但 1H27 有機會轉佳： 展望 2H26 、2027
年，預估 2H26 營收與 1H26 相去有限，但 2027 年則有成長空
間。若就產品結構而言，首先，雖然手機相關產品雖然必須面
對 2026 年整體手機市場需求因記憶體等零組件漲價、缺料衝擊
而降溫的負面大環境，但持續受惠於中國手機 PA 廠打入韓國
中階手機供應鏈、中國品牌手機高階機種所需 PA 代工地位穩
固，以及美國大客戶 (IC 設計公司 )對中國、韓國旗艦機滲透率
開始提高的衍生商機發揮。  
其次， Wi-Fi 相關產品 2H26 、 2027 年的動能則有三， (1)router 
Wi-Fi PA 訂單數量隨 Wi-Fi 7 市佔率提高而增加， (2) 無人機 PA 
1H26 營收 YoY+194.6% ， 由於客戶家數由 1~2 家增加至 4~5 家，
後續營收成長性仍高， (3) 受惠於美國 IDM 廠逐步退出中國市
場，美國大客戶、中國手機 PA 廠的手機 Wi-Fi PA 陸續打入中
國手機品牌廠的供應鏈，且多數訂單來自旗艦機、高階機種。  
不過，由於中、低階手機舊機種相關產品報價不易調整，旗艦
機、高階手機舊機種的漲價幅度亦難以全數反應原材料成本漲
幅，預估 2H26 宏捷科的毛利率仍受到原材料採購價格偏高影
響，惟待新機種相關產品陸續上市後，預估 1H27 毛利率即有機
會回升，獲利展望亦得以轉佳。

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 10 月 2 日

2 群益投顧 
  企業 ESG 資訊揭露：  
就溫室氣體管理策略而言，宏捷科的方向主要有六， (1) 配合政府環保政策
執行溫室氣體盤查， (2) 將空壓機安裝無耗氣排水器，減少運作電能消耗，
(3) 廠房照明設計以自然照明替代電能，並將高耗電之傳統燈具改為 LED 之
省電燈具 (持續改善中 )， (4) 廠內設備逐步汰換 IE3 等級以上之高效能馬達，
(5) 空壓機更換效能較佳的機台及降載， (6) 無塵室氣流循環驅動及空氣過濾
設備降載節能。  
再就溫室氣體排放減量之預算與計畫而言，宏捷科落實的途徑主要有五， (1)
生產程序優化， (2) 廠內節能措施進行， (3) 電力照明之替代及管理， (4) 馬達
汰舊換新 (IE3 等級以上 )， (5) 增設含氟排氣處理設備。  
至於宏捷科溫室氣體排放量減量目標則為針對電能減量以降低間接排放之
溫室氣體數量、減少 PFCs 氣體排放以減少直接排放之溫室氣體數量。  
手機相關產品出貨持續暢旺， 2Q26 稅後淨利 2.78 億元， QoQ-6.23% ：  
宏捷科為砷化鎵晶圓代工廠，目前總產能為 2 萬片 /月 (以 6 吋晶圓為計算基
礎 )，僅次於同業穩懋 (3105 TT) 的第二大廠，但就現有無塵室空間而言，最
大產能規模可達 4 萬片 /月，換言之，在市場需求增溫時，宏捷科僅需購置、
安裝機台，並通過客戶認證 (部份客戶甚至未要求認證 )後，即可擴大產能規
模。  
宏捷科的產品線包括 PA(power amplifier ，功率放大器 )、 switch/LNA( 開關
/low noise amplifier 低雜訊放大器 )等射頻 (RF，radio frequency) 產品，其中，
PA 多以 HBT 製程生產， switch/LNA 則多以 HEMT 製程生產，此外，宏捷
科亦生產光電 (optical) 產品線，主要為供 3D 感測光源之用的 VCSEL 。若再
就各產品線的終端應用而言，訂單則多來自 Wi-Fi 、手機 (phone) ，近幾年營
收比重分別為 30~60% 、 35~75% 。  
中美晶 (5483 TT) 於 08/2020 參與宏捷科的私募，宏捷科除與中美晶共同進
軍 GaN( 氮化鎵 )領域外，並可藉所取得的資金投入研發，至於中美晶則得以
在宏捷科量產的過程中供應自身生產的 GaN on SiC( 碳化矽基氮化鎵 )材料，
亦即雙方合作將發揮垂直整合綜效。  
2Q26 宏捷科營收 12.98 億元， QoQ+6.23% ，原因在於手機相關產品營收增
加至 9.49 億元， QoQ+7.55% ，不過，受到磊晶片 (epi wafer) 、黃金等原材料
漲價影響，毛利率降低至 29.98%(1Q26 為 34.63%) ，營業利益 QoQ-13.40% ，
稅後淨利 2.78 億元， QoQ-6.23% 。  
1H26 宏捷科營收 25.21 億元，為歷史次高， YoY+44.36% ，其中，手機相關
產品營收 18.32 億元， YoY+56.16% ，原因有二， (1) 中國手機 PA 廠打入韓
國中階手機供應鏈，且自 2026 年開始大量出貨 (過去中、高階手機用 PA 多
由美國業者供應 )， (2) 中國手機 PA 廠供中國手機品牌業者高階機種使用的
產品多仍交由宏捷科代工 (搭載於中、低階手機的 PA 則已積極轉單至中國
同業 )。  
2H26 毛利率仍受原物料成本偏高影響，但 1H27 獲利展望有機會轉佳：  
展望 2H26 、 2027 年，預估 2H26 營收與 1H26 相去有限，但 2027 年則有成

3 群益投顧 
長空間。若就產品結構而言，首先，雖然手機相關產品雖然必須面對 2026
年整體手機市場需求因記憶體等零組件漲價、缺料衝擊而降溫的負面大環
境，但持續受惠於中國手機 PA 廠打入韓國中階手機供應鏈、中國品牌手機
高階機種所需 PA 代工地位穩固，以及美國大客戶 (IC 設計公司 )對中國、韓
國旗艦機滲透率開始提高的衍生商機發揮。  
其次， Wi-Fi 相關產品 2H26 、 2027 年的動能則有三， (1)router Wi-Fi PA 訂
單數量隨 Wi-Fi 7 市佔率提高而增加 ，(2) 無人機 PA 1H26 營收 YoY+194.6% ，
由於客戶家數由 1~2 家增加至 4~5 家，後續營收成長性仍高， (3) 受惠於美
國 IDM 廠逐步退出中國市場，美國大客戶、中國手機 PA 廠的手機 Wi-Fi 
PA 陸續打入中國手機品牌廠的供應鏈，且多數訂單來自旗艦機、高階機種。
最後，在新產品的部份，包括 datacom 用 VCSEL( 後續亦將開發 PD 及更高
波長的產品 )、濾波器、消費型 LiDAR( 終端應用如車用、掃地機器人、割草
機等 )均已量產， solar cell 則將於 2026 年年底量產，惟對宏捷科的營收貢獻
程度則均尚待觀察。  
不過，由於中、低階手機舊機種相關產品報價不易調整，旗艦機、高階手機
舊機種的漲價幅度亦難以全數反應原材料成本漲幅，預估 2H26 宏捷科的毛
利率仍受到原材料採購價格偏高影響，惟待新機種相關產品陸續上市後，預
估 1H27 毛利率有機會回升，獲利展望亦得以轉佳。  
雖 2H26 獲利展望仍保守，但 1H27 有機會轉佳，群益對宏捷科的投資建議
為 Trading Buy ：  
由於磊晶片、黃金等原材料成本偏高，宏捷科 2H26 獲利表現不易較 2Q26
明顯改善，不過，待新機種相關產品陸續上市後，預估 1H27 毛利率有機會
回升，獲利展望亦得以轉佳，故群益對宏捷科的投資建議為 Trading Buy 。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Growth in multiples
We forecast revenue to more than double next year
Bloom Energy’s planned capacity expansion of up to 10GW strengthens Kaori’s
long-term SOFC growth outlook, while higher liquid cooling content and
shipments should sustain liquid cooling momentum into 2027. CSP CDU heat
exchangers and semiconductor equipment cooling could provide additional

growth drivers from 4Q26. We raise our 2027/28 EPS forecasts by 14%/12% and
increase our target price to NT$2,710 from NT$2,300. Reiterate HC O-PF rating.
Bloom Energy to double its capacity
Bloom Energy (BE) has announced plans to acquire a 158k sqft facility in F remont,
U
S, comparable in size to its existing Fremont manufacturing plant, which can
produce up to 5GW. We believe the acquisition could support an eventual doubling
of its fuel cell stack manufacturing capacity to 10GW. We expect its supply chain

to support shipments of 2/4/6GW in 2026/27/28 to meet strong solid oxide fuel
cell (SOFC). demand. To support this ramp, Kaori is expanding capacity to 6-7x its
current level by end-2027. We now forecast Kaori’s sales from BE to grow by
185%/66% YoY in 2027/28, versus our previous estimate of 101%/77%.
Strong liquid cooling momentum into 2027
With SpaceX planning an additional 6-8GW of datacentre capacity in 2027, we
b
elieve GPU server suppliers Supermicro and Dell will be key beneficiaries,

supporting continued growth across their supply chains from 2H26 into 2027.
Higher content value for Rubin manifolds, together with stronger CDU and rear-
door heat exchanger (RDHx) shipments, should support Kaori’s liquid cooling
momentum. We forecast its liquid cooling revenue to grow by 60%/25% YoY in
2027/28.
Diversifying the growth engines
While SOFC and Supermicro-related liquid cooling remain Kaori’s main growth
d
rivers, we believe plate heat exchangers for CSP CDUs and liquid cooling

applications in semiconductor equipment could emerge as additional growth drivers
from 4Q26. These opportunities should broaden Kaori’s customer and application
exposure, supporting its longer-term growth outlook.
Earnings revisions and valuation
We raise our 2027/28SP EPS forecasts by 14%/12% to reflect higher expected
d
emand and shipments for BE's fuel cells, alongside a stronger liquid cooling
outlook. We increase our TP to NT$2,710 from NT$2,300, based on an unchanged

40x 2H27-1H28SP PE. Reiterate High Conviction Outperform rating.

Investment thesis
Kaori is well positioned to capitalise on rising demand for liquid cooling, and it
expects related product shipments to ramp up. The fuel-cell business should be a
huge growth driver in 2026-27 to meet AI power infrastructure demand in the US.
In the longer term, we believe the company will also benefit from heat-pump
demand as Europe is phasing out gas boilers, despite some noise about near-term
demand. Kaori has been planning capacity expansion earlier than peers to fully

capture demand, with rising exposure to large clients. We thus expect the company
to enjoy robust earnings in coming years.

### Page 5

Growth in multiples Kaori - HC O-PF
6 October 2026 5
Catalysts
The major growth catalysts largely depend on fuel-cell projects for AI datacentres
taking off, increasing content value in liquid cooling offerings, and a clearer outlook
for hydrogen electrolyzer development.
Valuation details
We adopt a PE-based valuation methodology as we believe the market will mainly
focus on the earnings growth outlook, which is better reflected with PE metrics.

We base our valuation on 40x PE, which is around +1sd of its five-year average and
supported by an 93% EPS Cagr in 2026-28SP, to account for the surging demand
for AI server liquid cooling and power infrastructure. We are positive on the
company's long-term outlook given its growing stationary fuel cell demand from AI
datacentres, as well as its efforts to diversify customer exposure for liquid cooling
business. We believe the company can maintain its competitive edge over other

cooling players given its expertise in vacuum welding technology to manufacture
high-quality products.
Investment risks
Downside risks: 1) increased competition for Bloom Energy's fuel cell business or
new technology solutions replacing fuel cells; 2) slower kick-off of new fuel cell
projects for AI datacentres; 3) weaker-than-expected liquid cooling adoption; 4)
slower rollout of Europe/US government subsidies for heat pump installations.

## Document 158: AI驅動高速互聯，矽光子落地，CPO續章 202609

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI驅動高速互聯，矽光子落地，CPO續章 202609.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源

Pages: 32
Extraction status: ok

2026/09

### Page 2

2【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
結論
⚫ AI算力需求推升高速光互聯市場。美國四大CSP持續擴大AI資料中心建置，為帶動高速光
模組需求成長的主要動能。現階段高速光模組出貨仍以800G為主，1.6T預計自2027年逐步
放量，也帶動矽光(SiPh)模組滲透率提高。
⚫ 矽光模組非新興產品，外型仍沿用既有可插拔光模組規格，但相較傳統光模組，SiPh具備
元件高度整合及晶圓級量產優勢，隨高速傳輸所需而獲廣泛採用。然而，雷射晶片受限於
上游InP基板缺貨而供應吃緊。為確保貨源，部分業者已透過長約或預付款方式鎖定供應，
使掌握貨源者在市場上更具競爭優勢。
⚫ CPO現階段仍面臨光耦合、測試與量產等製程挑戰；對CSP而言，CPO改變其既有可插拔光
模組的維修與更換模式，加上NPO、XPO等替代架構持續發展，使CPO雖已進入量產階段
，短期滲透率仍偏低。展望未來，傳輸頻寬持續提升，為降低傳輸損耗與功率，光引擎將
逐步靠近交換器ASIC或加速卡，CPO仍具導入需求。此外，NVIDIA持續擴大NVLink

Fusion生態系，擴大CPO潛在應用市場，惟規模部署時程仍待帶動頻寬需求的新應用興起。
⚫ 相關個股：聯亞(3081)、IET-KY(4971)、寰宇-KY(4991)、聯鈞(3450)、訊芯-KY(6451)、華
星光(4979)、光聖(6442)、波若威(3163)、上詮(3363)、大立光(3008)

AI算力需求推升高速光互聯市場

### Page 5

5【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
AI DC建置，光通訊需求持續成長
• 生成式AI浪潮帶動美國CSP進入DC建置競賽，2023年仍為投資初期，2024年CapEx明顯加速
，預期2026年美國四大CSP—Amazon、Google、微軟、Meta ，CapEx合計增至7,500億美元，
帶動高速光互聯(networking)需求。
• Amazon更指出，預期AWS算力供不應求情況將延續至2027年，且2028年需求能見度已高。
• 2022年底ChatGPT推出帶動生成式AI浪潮，惟當時COVID-19疫情剛結束，Amazon和Meta因
疫情期間大幅擴張基礎建設而下調2023年CapEx，使四大CSP整體CapEx較2022年略低。
資料來源：福邦投顧彙整

### Page 6

6【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
預估2031年光模組市場營收將達1,120億美元
資料來源：Yole, 福邦投顧彙整
• 市調機構預估，至2031年光模組市場營收將達1,120億美元，CAGR成長30%。AI DC為營收
貢獻主力， 佔比達9成。
• 2025年合計資料中心與電信商的光模組營收為230億美元，當時兩者營收分別為180億與50億
美元，差異有限；2031年資料中心的光模組營收達1,010億，電信商僅增為110億美元。
圖2、2025與2031年光模組市場規模比較

### Page 7

7【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
光互連應用由機櫃延伸至跨資料中心
資料來源：Ciena, 福邦投顧彙整
圖3、資料中心Scale Up、Scale Out與Scale Across架構
• 目前光通訊產品出貨集中於Scale Up與Scale Out應用；隨資料中心建置規模擴大及耗電量增加
，受限於電力與土地供給，資料中心朝分散式建置發展，並透過光纖串聯多座資料中心以進行
共同運算，帶動Scale Across需求。
• Scale Up為機櫃內元件與設備互連；Scale Out為資料中心內不同機櫃間互連；Scale Across為
資料中心互連。

### Page 8

8【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
依傳輸距離採用不同光互聯技術
資料來源：Ciena, 福邦投顧彙整
• Scale Up傳輸距離較短，以成本較低的主動銅纜(ACC)為主；隨傳輸速率增加，銅線難以維
持訊號品質，便會採用光纖兩端配置光收發模組的主動光纜(AOC)。
• Scale Out主流採用IMDD可插拔(pluggable)光模組。
• NPO和CPO為應用於Scale Out和Scale up的新技術，藉縮短光引擎與交換器ASIC距離以降低
高速傳輸的電訊號損耗。NPO的光引擎鄰近交換器ASIC擺放；CPO的光引擎與交換器ASIC
共同封裝。
• Scale Across傳輸距離約20公里以上，需採用相干光(coherent)技術來維持訊號品質，供應商
相對有限，以歐美系業者為主。
圖4、三類架構的光互聯技術需求

10【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
AI資料中心的光互聯台廠供應鏈
• AI DC 高速互連升級，帶動 AOC/ACC 與800G/1.6T可插拔光模組需求成長，並推升矽光
(SiPh)模組滲透率。台廠AOC供應商包括佳必琪與源傑。
• 台廠受惠範圍由光模組元件與封裝，進一步延伸至CW Laser、光學次模組封裝及 PIC 代工
• 高密度光纖佈線需求同步增加，帶動 MPO/Jumper、Fiber Harness、Shuffle Module 等產品需
求，台廠供應商包括光聖、波若威、佳必琪、貿聯-KY。

### Page 11

11【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
光模組朝高速與高密度整合演進
• AI DC帶動高速光模組需求，2026年以400G、800G為主，1.6T開始放量；2027年1.6T出貨量
將明顯提升，並預期2028年導入3.2T。市調機構LightCounting預估，隨800G、1.6T等高速光
模組需求增加，矽光模組滲透率將提升，2026年矽光模組銷售額YoY成長110%，占整體光模
組市場約50%。
• 光模組速率提升是透過增加光通道或提高單通道速率來實現，以每通道200G為例，800G需4
個光通道，1.6T則增至8個，通道數增加所需的調變器、PD等元件數量也隨之增加，使耦合
及封裝複雜度提高，凸顯矽光模組的整合優勢
資料來源：LightCounting，千才，福邦投顧彙整

### Page 12

12【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
矽光PIC高度整合光元件並以半導體製程量產
• 矽光模組是指搭載矽光PIC的光模組，矽光PIC透過半導體製程，將調變器、波導等光學功能
整合於晶片，減少離散元件組裝及光學對準步驟，並有利於晶圓級量產。
• 2016年英特爾即推出矽光子光模組，惟早期高速傳輸需求有限，矽光PIC整合效益尚不顯著；
隨800G、1.6T以上高速光模組放量，通道數及光元件數量增加，矽光PIC整合優勢浮現。
• 現階段矽光模組仍沿用沿用OSFP、QSFP-DD既有可插拔光模組外型。
• 過去光通訊PIC多採III-V族材料，雷射與光元件可單晶整合，但市場規模較小且製程專用，多
以IDM方式生產；矽光PIC採CMOS相容製程，可導入成熟矽晶圓代工模式，實現規模量產與
降低成本，然無法與雷射整合。
資料來源：博通，福邦投顧彙整
圖7、傳統光模組與矽光子光模組架構比較

### Page 13

13【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
矽光PIC需求擴大，專業分工成形
• 矽光PIC晶圓代工目前以Tower半導體量產規模及客戶能見度最高；聯電亦積極切入，2025年
取得比利時半導體研究機構imec的12吋矽光子製程技術授權，2026年於新加坡12吋廠量產
1.6T PIC，預計2027年向更多客戶開放矽光代工平台。
• 除英特爾外，其餘具備矽光PIC設計能力業者並未單獨銷售PIC，而是將PIC與EIC、DSP整合
為光引擎(optical engine)後出售。
• 2023年英特爾出售光模組事業予Jabil，專注於提供整合雷射的矽光PIC。

表4、雷射供應商製程布局
• Lumentum、Cohernet、博通採IDM模式，具備從LD設計、磊晶、晶圓製程至雷射封裝能力，
並視產能與產品需求委外，如Coherent將部分LD封裝委由聯鈞生產。
• 磊晶製程方面，全新、聯亞採用MOCVD設備，英特磊以MBE為主，IQE同時具備MOCVD與
MBE量產能力。其中聯亞已切入國際龍頭光模組廠供應鏈，華星光與Marvell具合作關係。
• LD封裝包含CoS和To-Can等形式，聯鈞現階段以CoS為主，封裝後雷射可再與透鏡、光纖等
光學元件整合成TOSA；高功率CW雷射可用於矽光模組，或CPO/NPO的外部雷射光源。

### Page 18

CPO發展趨勢與導入挑戰

### Page 19

19【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
CPO於3.2T世代有望加速滲透
• 台積電表示CPO模組將於2026年下半年量產，惟依供應鏈資訊，初期出貨量仍低、市場規模
有限。市調機構預估，CPO光模組出貨量將自2027年起明顯成長，2030年達約6,000萬顆；
滲透率部分，將由2027年的11%提升至2028年的19%，2030年進一步達38%。
• 業界普遍認為，傳輸速率進入3.2T世代後，CPO在功耗與頻寬密度上的優勢將更為明顯。博
通也表示，CPO的光互聯耗電量較可插拔光模組減少70%。
資料來源：Lightcounting，Dell’Oro, 福邦投顧彙整
圖10、CPO出貨量與滲透率預估

### Page 20

20【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
CPO推動光電共封裝，FAU成光耦合關鍵元件
• CPO為光電共封裝架構，將 PIC與EIC 整合為光引擎(OE)，再與Switch ASIC共封裝於同一載
板，目標降低電訊號傳輸距離與功耗。
• 雷射仍放置於交換器機箱前面板，且維持可插拔，以方便更換。
• 目前台積電以COUPE平台整合PIC與EIC成光引擎，然提高光耦合效率、降低光進入 PIC 的
損耗，成為CPO主要瓶頸之一，也使 FAU 成為關鍵元件。
資料來源：Photonics Spectra, 福邦投顧彙整

### Page 21

21【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
FAU難以晶片化，攸關CPO光耦合效率與量產時程
• 目前台積電COUPE平台優先採用光柵(grating)耦合架構，FAU以黏膠固定於光學引擎上
方。光線平行射出，需以反射鏡導入PIC；COUPE端利用矽透鏡將光聚焦至光柵耦合器
，也配置金屬反射鏡回收散射光，提升光耦合效率。
• 光耦合效率攸關光學元件設計與光纖精密對位。FAU除涉及光學設計外，光纖亦需精準
置入V-groove，並完成固定、端面研磨與對位封裝，工序繁雜而被視為CPO規模量產瓶
頸之一。
• FAU投入業者主要包括線材廠、傳統FAU業者及光學元件廠。例如，光聖旗下合聖推出
以Metalens搭配反射鏡的可拆卸式FAU；大立光則推出配置80顆微型鏡頭、整合反射鏡
與稜鏡功能的PMLA，並進一步布局多層堆疊光纖。線材廠方面，以波若威、佳必琪與
貿聯為代表；傳統FAU業者則以上銓為代表。
資料來源：TSMC, 福邦投顧彙整
圖12、TSMC COUPE的FAU光耦合架構
大立光、合聖針對反射鏡推出解決方案

### Page 22

22【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
FAU技術升級聚焦提升耦光效率與縮短對位時間
• FAU主要發展方向為可拆卸設計、多層光纖堆疊與被動對位。其中，可拆卸式方案以美、日
業者布局較積極，主要考量FAU或光纖生損壞可直接更換，避免拆除整組光學引擎，降低維
修難度及交換器損壞風險。
• 目前FAU多採主動對位，透過反覆微調位置並量測光功率，找出最佳耦合點後固定，雖可補
償製造與組裝誤差，但組裝時間長。被動對位仰賴高精度製程與定位結構，將精度要求前移
，以簡化後段組裝並提升量產效率
資料來源：各業者, 福邦投顧彙整
圖13、FAU正發展可拆卸、多層堆疊及被動對準
日商Senko的可拆卸FAU
Teramount雙層光纖設計使
光纖量從單層的32條增為
64條。
Teramount與高塔半導體合作開發Bump-ready wafer，
即在PIC晶圓階段預先製作光學耦合與對位結構。

### Page 23

23【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
NPO持續演進 成為Scale Up另一選擇
• NPO被視為CPO折衷方案，光引擎置於鄰近交換器ASIC，降低傳輸損耗且保留維修彈性。
• 華為認為NPO具備提供400G/lane的潛力，3.2T世代未必需全面導入CPO。
• 博通、Marvell、Coherent皆已展示或推出NPO方案，光源架構部分，Marvell主打無DSP、配
置CW雷射；博通展示VCSEL方案，Coherent同時推出VCSEL和CW方案，VCSEL功耗低，
晶片尺寸小，可高密度排列，並與光引擎整合封裝，然傳輸距離有限，適合Scale-up應用。
• 被視為Marvell合作業者的華星光，預期2027年下半開始量產1.6T NPO模組，2028年出貨量
體將較為顯著。
資料來源：華為, 福邦投顧彙整
圖14、NPO與CPO技術路徑比較

### Page 24

24【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
XPO重塑可插拔模組 挑戰CPO的Scale Out市場
• 美網通業者Arista於2026年成立XPO MSA，並展示首代支援200G/lane，頻寬12.8T的可插拔
XPO模組，後續將發展400G/lane、單模組頻寬25.6T，惟量產與規模商用時程尚未明朗。
XPO MSA成員橫跨CSP、ASIC、光元件與光模組供應鏈，包括微軟、Marvell、Coherent、
Lumentum及新易盛等。Arista為Ethernet交換器設備商，主導XPO規格與生態系發展。
• XPO主打Scale-out應用，強調單一XPO模組可取代8顆OSFP，在有限機箱空間內提高整體傳
輸頻寬。由於XPO採全新模組機構，交換器須配合重新設計供電、散熱及相關介面，目前規
格制定與系統平台仍以Arista為主；儘管XPO MSA讓CSP在光模組供應商選擇上更具彈性，
但在其他交換器業者尚未跟進前，導入XPO亦可能意味對Arista交換器平台的更高依賴。
資料來源：福邦投顧彙整

圖15、XPO模組架構與應用示意

### Page 25

25【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
Open CPX以標準化介面挑戰NVIDIA封閉式CPO架構
• 美系光傳輸設備商Ciena與Coherent，以及連接器業者Molex、Samtec推動Open CPX MSA，
透過標準介面讓讓光引擎可拆卸，獨立於ASIC，提高光引擎與ASIC的選擇彈性。
• Open CPX強調架構選擇彈性，CSP可依系統需求採用NPO或CPO，雷射可選外部雷射或整合
式雷射方案。
• Ciena已推出採Open CPX架構的光引擎Vesta，，並以16 顆6.4T光引擎打造總頻寬102.4T的系
統。Ciena於3Q26法說表示，Vesta樣品已售出，預計2027年開始貢獻營收、2028年放量。
資料來源：Ciena，福邦投顧彙整
圖16、Open CPX採可拆卸光引擎設計，提升供應商選擇彈性
強調光模組配置支援CPX
規格的連接器，實現可拆卸OpenCPX成員可見到連接器廠商如
Molex 和Samtec。

### Page 26

26【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
各種光互聯架構重塑供應鏈價值分配
資料來源：福邦投顧彙整
受惠供應鏈 承壓供應鏈
CPO/NPO 光引擎、外部雷射、FAU 傳統可插拔光模組
XPO 可插拔光模組、雷射 相對有限
Open CPX 可拆卸光引擎、連接器、雷射、FAU 封閉式CPO供應鏈
• CPO和NPO的供應鏈差異有限，皆由交換器ASIC或加速卡業者主導系統架構，光引擎、雷
射、FAU、連接器等供應鏈皆配合設計。若CPO滲透率提高，將壓縮傳統可插拔光模組需求
，但具雷射組裝能力的業者仍可供應外部雷射
• CPO的光耦合效率需求提高，使FAU重要性提升。
• XPO藉提高模組頻寬，延續可插拔方案在高速傳輸的生命週期。
• Open CPX採用標準介面實現光引擎可拆卸，由於PCB板端需配置CPX連接器，使連接器重
要性提升。
• 不論採用何種光互連架構，隨AI資料中心傳輸頻寬與光路數持續增加，高密度佈線需求均將
提升，帶動fiber jumper、fiber harness及fiber shuffle需求，如光聖已切入Google資料中心光纖

連接供應鏈將明顯受惠。
表5、不同光互連架構受惠與承壓供應鏈比較

### Page 27

27【僅供內部教育訓練使用，嚴禁外流 】
CPO長期具導入需求，惟時程仍具不確定性
• CPO在Scale-out面臨傳統可插拔光模組、XPO與NPO等架構競爭；相較下，台積電認為CPO
在Scale-up更能發揮高頻寬、低功耗與短距離互連優勢，朝Optical I/O發展。
• Scale-up 架構目前存在技術路線分歧—Narrow & Fast 與 Wide & Slow；前者持續提高單通道
速率，以NVIDIA為代表，後者增加道數來擴充頻寬，微軟MOSAIC為較典型方案。
• 展望未來，儘管CPO導入時程受系統架構與成本效益影響，但隨運算規模持續擴大，銅互連
瓶頸將推動光引擎進一步靠近交換器ASIC或XPU，CPO長期導入趨勢不變。 再者，NVIDIA
亦透過NVLink Fusion持續擴大Fabric生態系，使採用客製XPU的業者仍可導入NVLink架構
，擴大CPO潛在應用市場。
資料來源：TSMC, 福邦投顧彙整
圖17、CPO應用場域與Scale-up技術路線
應用於Scale- out 應用於Scale- up Scale- up技術路線
